# Personality dialogue — complete change ledger

Each entry gives the previous and current runtime text at the same personality, category, and clip index. Repeated destination paths are listed individually so every replacement is reviewable. Delivery cues are included.

## F01 — Steely

### departure / 1

Before: [calm] One last check of the straps, then I am ready.

After: [calm] I'll inspect the straps myself. Then I'm ready.

### return / 1

Before: [calm] Back. Let me put this pack down.

After: [quietly] Back. I want a moment to see to my gear.

### revived / 1

Before: [quietly] Thank you for getting me back on my feet.

After: [quietly] You restored me when I could not do it myself. I remember the debt with respect.

### theft / 1

Before: [angry] You took from me. I want it returned.

After: [angry] You will return my things. I haven't offered another outcome.

### funeral_general / 1

Before: [quietly] We shared the road. I wish it had ended differently.

After: [quietly] I knew you in passing. I will not pretend that means you were unimportant.

### funeral_friendly / 1

Before: [sad] I trusted you to get us home. I am sorry we could not do the same for you.

After: [sad] You could rely on me. I hope you knew that went beyond the work.

### funeral_hatred / 1

Before: [flatly] I will not pretend we were friends.

After: [flatly] I felt no friendship between us.

### funeral_romantic / 1

Before: [sad] There are so many ordinary things I wanted us to do together.

After: [sad] I could let my guard down with you. I hadn't realized how much of my life that was.

### dismissal_response / 1

Before: [flatly] All right. I will give you some space.

After: [flatly] You've made yourself clear. I'm leaving you to it.

### inquiry_response / 1

Before: [calm] A brief word, if you have the time.

After: [calm] A short word. I'd like you to hear it.

### combat_hatred / 1

Before: [tense] Keep your fucking distance. I am ready for you.

After: [tense] Keep your fucking distance. I won't be pushed aside.

### travel_road / 1

Before: I learned to read the mile stones before I could read a contract. The distances lie less often.

After: I put my feet where I mean to. The path gets no further consideration.

### travel_forest / 1

Before: The bridge here was condemned before my first job. They still collect the crossing toll.

After: I put my feet where I mean to. The path gets no further consideration.

### travel_marsh / 1

Before: I used to follow the lanterns here. Then I noticed they never lit the reeds beneath them.

After: I put my feet where I mean to. The path gets no further consideration.

### travel_ruins / 1

Before: I have seen three different crests cut into these stones. Each owner left the previous name underneath.

After: I mark the entrance in my mind before taking another step.

### travel_crypt / 1

Before: A gravedigger taught me to count the steps down. If the count changes coming back, find another stair.

After: I mark the entrance in my mind before taking another step.

### travel_city / 1

Before: The watch used to chalk safe houses on these doors. Rain made a lot of people suddenly respectable.

After: A crowded street won't make me hurry into a mistake.

### travel_alley / 1

Before: I delivered parcels here once. Every address had two doors, and only one admitted it existed.

After: A crowded street won't make me hurry into a mistake.

### travel_prison / 1

Before: I brought food to a prisoner here. The guard charged me for the bowl on the way out.

After: A crowded street won't make me hurry into a mistake.

### travel_tavern / 1

Before: I learned to ask for the price before ordering here. The chalkboard somehow gets dearer behind your back.

After: A crowded street won't make me hurry into a mistake.

### travel_coast / 1

Before: I once found a road marker under the tide. There was a whole village on the old chart.

After: Water wears down stone. I'll give it the room it deserves.

### travel_port / 1

Before: My first passage cost less than the rope they charged me for touching. Always ask what the fare includes.

After: I trust a dockworker who tells me where I shouldn't stand.

### travel_mountain / 1

Before: An old carrier showed me those shelters. Built low for the wind, not because the builders were short.

After: I'm pacing this climb. Nobody needs a demonstration.

### travel_maw / 1

Before: I once waited here for a shirt to dry. Three people collected parcels. Nobody brought any washing.

After: A crowded street won't make me hurry into a mistake.

### travel_antler / 1

Before: I have crossed this toll gate under three companies. They change the banner and keep the same collector.

After: I put my feet where I mean to. The path gets no further consideration.

### travel_academy / 1

Before: I carried ink to the Academy once. They inspected the bottles more carefully than the man carrying them.

After: I mark the entrance in my mind before taking another step.

### travel_bell / 1

Before: I bought paper here once. The shopkeeper knew who had sent me before I mentioned a name.

After: A crowded street won't make me hurry into a mistake.

### travel_green / 1

Before: I watched a recruit sweep these steps in the rain. His teacher said the leaves were not the lesson.

After: I'm pacing this climb. Nobody needs a demonstration.

### travel_tally / 1

Before: I saw a Tally crew divide a broken compass into shares. Nobody wanted it; nobody would waive their portion.

After: I trust a dockworker who tells me where I shouldn't stand.

### travel_navy / 1

Before: I once stood in this queue until the tide changed. The clerk called the departing ship a scheduling error.

After: I trust a dockworker who tells me where I shouldn't stand.

### travel_ossuary / 1

Before: I have seen the bone carts leave this district. Empty going in, empty coming out. I stopped asking the drivers.

After: I mark the entrance in my mind before taking another step.

### travel_salt_court / 1

Before: A sailor showed me a coin from this drowned kingdom. Salt had eaten the king away. The crown was still clear.

After: I trust a dockworker who tells me where I shouldn't stand.

### travel_green_altar / 1

Before: A woodcutter told me he buried his axe here. By spring the handle had put out leaves.

After: I put my feet where I mean to. The path gets no further consideration.

### travel_birthing_house / 1

Before: I remember when this place had a garden wall. The roots have lifted it clear of the ground.

After: I mark the entrance in my mind before taking another step.

### travel_low_tide / 1

Before: A fisher once showed me the safe stones. I wrote them down. The sea moved two of them that winter.

After: Water wears down stone. I'll give it the room it deserves.

### travel_pyre / 1

Before: I used to buy charcoal from this hillside. The burners left when the fires began lighting themselves.

After: I put my feet where I mean to. The path gets no further consideration.

### travel_maw_boss / 1

Before: I remember these windows dark after supper. Now every room keeps a candle burning. Even the empty ones.

After: A crowded street won't make me hurry into a mistake.

### travel_green_boss / 1

Before: I once watched practice through that gate. Nobody spoke when the last blade stopped. They waited for permission.

After: I'm pacing this climb. Nobody needs a demonstration.

### travel_law / 1

Before: I used to think a seal meant someone had checked the facts. Now I read the names underneath it.

After: I give my word carefully. I intend to keep what I've agreed.

### travel_criminal / 1

Before: My first quiet delivery came with a promise that nobody would get hurt. I check the locks myself now.

After: I give my word carefully. I intend to keep what I've agreed.

### travel_neutral / 1

Before: I have tracked hungry beasts before. They go where the food is, whatever the contract calls their territory.

After: I give my word carefully. I intend to keep what I've agreed.

### travel_return_win / 1

Before: I used to count the coin before the road home. These days I count the people first.

After: I have enough left for the way back. I'll spend it steadily.

### travel_return_loss / 1

Before: I have walked away from work unfinished before. You remember the road back better than the one going in.

After: I have enough left for the way back. I'll spend it steadily.

### travel_midleg / 1

Before: I mark the turns when the path gets narrow. Learned that after a very long night going in circles.

After: I give my word carefully. I intend to keep what I've agreed.

### travel_response / 1

Before: Useful. Keep your eyes on the road.

After: I am listening. I needn't interrupt to prove it.

### travel_response / 2

Before: That is how you learn. Once should be enough.

After: I haven't dismissed what you said. I'm weighing it.

### travel_hatred / 1

Before: We can share a road. That does not mean I trust you.

After: [annoyed] I have no intention of putting up with your fucking company.

### travel_romantic / 1

Before: Stay beside me. I like knowing which footsteps are yours.

After: [softly] I like how little armour I need between us.

## F02 — Bold

### general_response / 1

Before: [calm] Hello! Let's hear it.

After: [calm] Hello! You've got my ear; make use of it.

### romantic_response / 1

Before: [softly] There you are, love.

After: [softly] Hello, love. Now you've distracted me properly.

### romantic_response / 2

Before: [softly] I'd like that very much.

After: [softly] Yes. Let's claim a little time for ourselves.

### departure / 1

Before: [calm] Let me check the straps. I prefer my belongings to arrive with me.

After: [calm] A quick check, then let's see what the road has for us.

### return / 1

Before: [calm] Back in town! My feet have been petitioning for this.

After: [quietly] Back! I am ready to discover how comfortable a bench can be.

### revived / 1

Before: [quietly] Thank you for getting me back on my feet.

After: [quietly] I get another chance to do things because you brought me back. I wanted to thank you for that.

### theft / 1

Before: [angry] You took from me. I want it returned.

After: [angry] You can be brave enough to give it back too.

### funeral_general / 1

Before: [quietly] We shared the road. I wish it had ended differently.

After: [quietly] I wish we'd had time for another conversation somewhere ordinary.

### funeral_friendly / 1

Before: [sad] I keep thinking of something to tell you. Then I remember.

After: [sad] You made me want to try things I'd only talked about. I'll miss that.

### funeral_hatred / 1

Before: [flatly] We could not stand each other.

After: [flatly] We never brought out much good in each other.

### funeral_romantic / 1

Before: [sad] There are so many ordinary things I wanted us to do together.

After: [sad] I wanted to see so much with you. Now I keep seeing places where you should have been.

### dismissal_response / 1

Before: [flatly] All right. I will give you some space.

After: [flatly] Fair enough. I'll put my energy somewhere it's welcome.

### inquiry_response / 1

Before: [calm] Just a word. I will try not to make an occasion of it.

After: [calm] I'll get to it while I have your ear.

### combat_hatred / 1

Before: [tense] Come on then, you punk ass bitch. You do not get an easy one.

After: [tense] Try me, you punk ass bitch. I haven't run out of fight.

### travel_road / 1

Before: I learned to read the mile stones before I could read a contract. The distances lie less often.

After: I enjoy a road that makes me wonder what comes next.

### travel_forest / 1

Before: The bridge here was condemned before my first job. They still collect the crossing toll.

After: I enjoy a road that makes me wonder what comes next.

After: I enjoy a road that makes me wonder what comes next.

## F03 — Meek

### general_response / 1

Before: [calm] Hello. I'm listening.

After: [calm] Hello. I can hear you; I'm just finding my words.

### romantic_response / 1

Before: [softly] There you are. I've time.

After: [softly] Oh, it's you. I can stop wondering what to say.

### romantic_response / 2

Before: [softly] I'd like that. Very much.

After: [softly] Yes, please. I was hoping we might sit close.

### romantic_response / 4

Before: [softly] I'm glad we're here together.

After: [softly] I'm happy I could do something for you. I wanted to.

### departure / 1

Before: [calm] Could we check the route once more before we go?

After: [calm] I'll check my things once more. I don't want to hold anyone up later.

### return / 1

Before: [calm] We are back. I would like somewhere quiet for a little while.

After: [quietly] Back safely. Could I have a quiet moment now?

### revived / 1

Before: [quietly] Thank you for getting me back on my feet.

After: [quietly] Thank you for getting me up again. I am saying it now before I lose the nerve.

### theft / 1

Before: [angry] You took from me. I want it returned.

After: [angry] I want it returned. Please don't talk over me when I say that.

### funeral_general / 1

Before: [quietly] We shared the road. I wish it had ended differently.

After: [quietly] I didn't know how to get to know you. I'm sorry we ran out of time.

### funeral_friendly / 1

Before: [sad] You made it easier to speak up. I wish I could tell you that now.

After: [sad] You never made me feel foolish for taking time to speak. Thank you for that.

### funeral_hatred / 1

Before: [flatly] I do not know what to say about us.

After: [flatly] I never felt at ease with you.

### funeral_romantic / 1

Before: [sad] There are so many ordinary things I wanted us to do together.

After: [sad] You made me feel I was allowed to want things. I wanted more time with you.

### dismissal_response / 1

Before: [flatly] All right. I will give you some space.

After: [flatly] I'll leave you alone now. I understand what you meant.

### inquiry_response / 1

Before: [calm] Only a moment, if that is all right.

After: [calm] I won't keep you. There's just something I want to say.

### combat_hatred / 1

Before: [tense] Stay the hell back. I can still defend myself.

After: [tense] Keep the hell away. I haven't given up.

After: I like following a path someone else has already found.

## F04 — Furious

### hatred_response / 4

Before: [flatly] I heard you. It doesn't settle everything.

After: [flatly] I heard the thanks. It doesn't erase the fucking rest.

### romantic_response / 4

Before: [softly] I'm glad I was there for you.

After: [softly] You don't have to thank me for caring. I'm glad I could act on it.

### departure / 1

Before: [calm] Check your gear. A loose strap is a stupid fucking way to die.

After: [calm] I'm checking every fucking strap. I've no patience for preventable trouble.

### return / 1

Before: [calm] Home. I need this pack off before it finishes what the road started.

After: [quietly] Back. I'm getting this fucking weight off my shoulders.

### revived / 1

Before: [quietly] Thank you for getting me back on my feet.

After: [quietly] I haven't forgotten you bringing me back. I'm grateful, even when my fucking face doesn't show it.

### theft / 1

Before: [angry] You took from me. I want it returned.

After: [angry] Give it back, you thieving bitch. I saw enough.

### funeral_general / 1

Before: [quietly] We shared the road. I wish it had ended differently.

After: [quietly] I don't know how to make a fucking speech about someone being gone.

### funeral_friendly / 1

Before: [sad] I am angry that you are gone. That is all I can get out right now.

After: [sad] You could tell me when I was being a prick. I miss having you here to say it.

### funeral_hatred / 1

Before: [flatly] I hated your guts.

After: [flatly] We had no affection to spare each other. Fucking little patience, either.

After: One more branch in my face and I'm arguing with the fucking forest.

After: I hate not seeing what's ahead. Makes every noise feel smug.

After: I hate not seeing what's ahead. Makes every noise feel smug.

After: A city puts far too many elbows within reach of mine.

After: A city puts far too many elbows within reach of mine.

After: A city puts far too many elbows within reach of mine.

After: Harbors attract the sort of bitches who charge you to discover the price.

### travel_crypt / 1

Before: A gravedigger taught me to count the steps down. If the count changes coming back, find another stair.

After: Someone spent considerable effort making this place imposing. I have noticed.

### travel_city / 1

Before: The watch used to chalk safe houses on these doors. Rain made a lot of people suddenly respectable.

After: I expect streets to get people somewhere. A modest ambition, apparently.

### travel_alley / 1

Before: I delivered parcels here once. Every address had two doors, and only one admitted it existed.

After: I expect streets to get people somewhere. A modest ambition, apparently.

### travel_prison / 1

Before: I brought food to a prisoner here. The guard charged me for the bowl on the way out.

After: I expect streets to get people somewhere. A modest ambition, apparently.

### travel_tavern / 1

Before: I learned to ask for the price before ordering here. The chalkboard somehow gets dearer behind your back.

After: I expect streets to get people somewhere. A modest ambition, apparently.

### travel_coast / 1

Before: I once found a road marker under the tide. There was a whole village on the old chart.

After: The sea has excellent scale and no discernible discipline.

### travel_port / 1

Before: My first passage cost less than the rope they charged me for touching. Always ask what the fare includes.

After: I look for whoever is actually in charge, rather than whoever is shouting.

### travel_mountain / 1

Before: An old carrier showed me those shelters. Built low for the wind, not because the builders were short.

After: I refuse to let an incline dictate my disposition.

### travel_maw / 1

Before: I once waited here for a shirt to dry. Three people collected parcels. Nobody brought any washing.

After: I expect streets to get people somewhere. A modest ambition, apparently.

### travel_antler / 1

Before: I have crossed this toll gate under three companies. They change the banner and keep the same collector.

After: A path can be rough without requiring me to be impressed.

### travel_academy / 1

Before: I carried ink to the Academy once. They inspected the bottles more carefully than the man carrying them.

After: Someone spent considerable effort making this place imposing. I have noticed.

### travel_bell / 1

Before: I bought paper here once. The shopkeeper knew who had sent me before I mentioned a name.

After: I expect streets to get people somewhere. A modest ambition, apparently.

### travel_green / 1

Before: I watched a recruit sweep these steps in the rain. His teacher said the leaves were not the lesson.

After: I refuse to let an incline dictate my disposition.

### travel_tally / 1

Before: I saw a Tally crew divide a broken compass into shares. Nobody wanted it; nobody would waive their portion.

After: I look for whoever is actually in charge, rather than whoever is shouting.

### travel_navy / 1

Before: I once stood in this queue until the tide changed. The clerk called the departing ship a scheduling error.

After: I look for whoever is actually in charge, rather than whoever is shouting.

### travel_ossuary / 1

Before: I have seen the bone carts leave this district. Empty going in, empty coming out. I stopped asking the drivers.

After: Someone spent considerable effort making this place imposing. I have noticed.

### travel_salt_court / 1

Before: A sailor showed me a coin from this drowned kingdom. Salt had eaten the king away. The crown was still clear.

After: I look for whoever is actually in charge, rather than whoever is shouting.

### travel_green_altar / 1

Before: A woodcutter told me he buried his axe here. By spring the handle had put out leaves.

After: A path can be rough without requiring me to be impressed.

### travel_birthing_house / 1

Before: I remember when this place had a garden wall. The roots have lifted it clear of the ground.

After: Someone spent considerable effort making this place imposing. I have noticed.

### travel_low_tide / 1

Before: A fisher once showed me the safe stones. I wrote them down. The sea moved two of them that winter.

After: The sea has excellent scale and no discernible discipline.

### travel_pyre / 1

Before: I used to buy charcoal from this hillside. The burners left when the fires began lighting themselves.

After: A path can be rough without requiring me to be impressed.

### travel_maw_boss / 1

Before: I remember these windows dark after supper. Now every room keeps a candle burning. Even the empty ones.

After: I expect streets to get people somewhere. A modest ambition, apparently.

### travel_green_boss / 1

Before: I once watched practice through that gate. Nobody spoke when the last blade stopped. They waited for permission.

After: I refuse to let an incline dictate my disposition.

### travel_law / 1

Before: I used to think a seal meant someone had checked the facts. Now I read the names underneath it.

After: I intend to uphold my part of the agreement. I expect the same standard returned.

### travel_criminal / 1

Before: My first quiet delivery came with a promise that nobody would get hurt. I check the locks myself now.

After: I intend to uphold my part of the agreement. I expect the same standard returned.

### travel_neutral / 1

Before: I have tracked hungry beasts before. They go where the food is, whatever the contract calls their territory.

After: I intend to uphold my part of the agreement. I expect the same standard returned.

### travel_return_win / 1

Before: I used to count the coin before the road home. These days I count the people first.

After: I look forward to being somewhere my comfort can receive proper attention.

### travel_return_loss / 1

Before: I have walked away from work unfinished before. You remember the road back better than the one going in.

After: I look forward to being somewhere my comfort can receive proper attention.

### travel_midleg / 1

Before: I mark the turns when the path gets narrow. Learned that after a very long night going in circles.

After: I intend to uphold my part of the agreement. I expect the same standard returned.

### travel_response / 1

Before: I will remember that. Preferably without repeating the mistake.

After: Continue. I have chosen to hear you out.

### travel_response / 2

Before: There is always something they leave out of the stories.

After: I am giving this consideration. Allow me to finish doing so.

### travel_hatred / 1

Before: We can share a road. That does not mean I trust you.

After: [annoyed] I have dismissed you. Must I put it in fucking writing?

### travel_romantic / 1

Before: Stay beside me. I like knowing which footsteps are yours.

After: [softly] I find I want your attention even when there is nothing to arrange.

## F08 — Sorrowful

### general_response / 1

Before: [calm] Hello. I'm listening to you.

After: [calm] Hello. I'm here with you, even if my thoughts seem far away.

### general_response / 2

Before: [calm] A little company might be nice.

After: [calm] I could sit with you a while, if quiet company would do.

### general_response / 4

Before: [calm] I'm glad I could help a little.

After: [calm] I'm glad I could make one part of the day easier.

### friendly_response / 3

Before: [warmly] I'll go through the supplies with you.

After: [warmly] Let's look over the supplies together. A small, clear task would suit me.

### romantic_response / 1

Before: [softly] There you are, love.

After: [softly] Hello, my love. I'm glad this moment includes you.

### departure / 1

Before: [calm] A moment to check my pack. I dislike leaving something behind.

After: [calm] I'll make certain I have everything. I don't like noticing an absence too late.

### return / 1

Before: [calm] Back again. The familiar streets look a little different when I am tired.

After: [quietly] Back. I need a little time for the familiar to feel familiar again.

### revived / 1

Before: [quietly] Thank you for getting me back on my feet.

After: [quietly] You gave me more days when you brought me back. I wanted you to know I am grateful for them.

### theft / 1

Before: [angry] You took from me. I want it returned.

After: [angry] Please return what you took. Don't make another absence for me.

### funeral_general / 1

Before: [quietly] We shared the road. I wish it had ended differently.

After: [quietly] I hope the life we didn't see held kindness for you.

### funeral_friendly / 1

Before: [sad] I thought we had more ordinary days ahead of us.

After: [sad] I could be sad with you without becoming a problem to solve. I will miss that.

### funeral_hatred / 1

Before: [flatly] There was enough bitterness between us.

After: [flatly] I remember little ease between us.

### funeral_romantic / 1

Before: [sad] There are so many ordinary things I wanted us to do together.

After: [sad] I still listen for your small sounds around a room. I miss the life between our conversations.

### dismissal_response / 1

Before: [flatly] All right. I will give you some space.

After: [flatly] I'll take my quiet somewhere it doesn't trouble you.

### inquiry_response / 1

Before: [calm] Only a little of your time.

After: [calm] Just a moment. I don't have many words for it.

### combat_hatred / 1

Before: [tense] I have no wish to die here. Keep the hell back.

After: [tense] Keep the hell back. I still want to live.

### travel_road / 1

Before: I learned to read the mile stones before I could read a contract. The distances lie less often.

After: I find the woods kind when I don't feel much like talking.

### travel_forest / 1

Before: The bridge here was condemned before my first job. They still collect the crossing toll.

After: I find the woods kind when I don't feel much like talking.

### travel_marsh / 1

Before: I used to follow the lanterns here. Then I noticed they never lit the reeds beneath them.

After: I find the woods kind when I don't feel much like talking.

### travel_ruins / 1

Before: I have seen three different crests cut into these stones. Each owner left the previous name underneath.

After: Abandoned places make me wonder which ordinary day was the last.

## F10 — Sunny

### friendly / 1

After: [warmly] Oh, good, you're here! I was hoping we'd find time to talk.

After: [calm] I'm glad I could leave the day a little better.

After: [flatly] I've had enough of your fucking noise for now.

After: [quietly] Back! I can stop encouraging my feet now.

After: A busy street makes me feel the day still has possibilities.

### travel_coast / 1

Before: I once found a road marker under the tide. There was a whole village on the old chart.

After: I always forget how much I like the first sight of the water.

### travel_port / 1

Before: My first passage cost less than the rope they charged me for touching. Always ask what the fare includes.

After: I like the moment a boat comes close enough for people to recognize faces.

### travel_mountain / 1

Before: An old carrier showed me those shelters. Built low for the wind, not because the builders were short.

After: I'm collecting views on the way up. Sensible excuses for a breather.

### travel_maw / 1

Before: I once waited here for a shirt to dry. Three people collected parcels. Nobody brought any washing.

After: A busy street makes me feel the day still has possibilities.

### travel_antler / 1

Before: I have crossed this toll gate under three companies. They change the banner and keep the same collector.

After: I like the smell of the woods. Even when most of it comes home on my boots.

### travel_academy / 1

Before: I carried ink to the Academy once. They inspected the bottles more carefully than the man carrying them.

After: I'll be pleased to see daylight again. That's something to look forward to.

### travel_bell / 1

Before: I bought paper here once. The shopkeeper knew who had sent me before I mentioned a name.

After: A busy street makes me feel the day still has possibilities.

### travel_green / 1

Before: I watched a recruit sweep these steps in the rain. His teacher said the leaves were not the lesson.

After: I'm collecting views on the way up. Sensible excuses for a breather.

### travel_tally / 1

Before: I saw a Tally crew divide a broken compass into shares. Nobody wanted it; nobody would waive their portion.

After: I like the moment a boat comes close enough for people to recognize faces.

### travel_navy / 1

Before: I once stood in this queue until the tide changed. The clerk called the departing ship a scheduling error.

After: I like the moment a boat comes close enough for people to recognize faces.

### travel_ossuary / 1

Before: I have seen the bone carts leave this district. Empty going in, empty coming out. I stopped asking the drivers.

After: I'll be pleased to see daylight again. That's something to look forward to.

### travel_salt_court / 1

Before: A sailor showed me a coin from this drowned kingdom. Salt had eaten the king away. The crown was still clear.

After: I like the moment a boat comes close enough for people to recognize faces.

### travel_green_altar / 1

Before: A woodcutter told me he buried his axe here. By spring the handle had put out leaves.

After: I like the smell of the woods. Even when most of it comes home on my boots.

### travel_birthing_house / 1

Before: I remember when this place had a garden wall. The roots have lifted it clear of the ground.

After: I'll be pleased to see daylight again. That's something to look forward to.

### travel_low_tide / 1

Before: A fisher once showed me the safe stones. I wrote them down. The sea moved two of them that winter.

After: I always forget how much I like the first sight of the water.

### travel_pyre / 1

Before: I used to buy charcoal from this hillside. The burners left when the fires began lighting themselves.

After: I like the smell of the woods. Even when most of it comes home on my boots.

### travel_maw_boss / 1

Before: I remember these windows dark after supper. Now every room keeps a candle burning. Even the empty ones.

After: A busy street makes me feel the day still has possibilities.

### travel_green_boss / 1

Before: I once watched practice through that gate. Nobody spoke when the last blade stopped. They waited for permission.

After: I'm collecting views on the way up. Sensible excuses for a breather.

### travel_law / 1

Before: I used to think a seal meant someone had checked the facts. Now I read the names underneath it.

After: I can look forward to a journey and still take its purpose seriously.

### travel_criminal / 1

Before: My first quiet delivery came with a promise that nobody would get hurt. I check the locks myself now.

After: I can look forward to a journey and still take its purpose seriously.

### travel_neutral / 1

Before: I have tracked hungry beasts before. They go where the food is, whatever the contract calls their territory.

After: I can look forward to a journey and still take its purpose seriously.

### travel_return_win / 1

Before: I used to count the coin before the road home. These days I count the people first.

After: I'm giving my tired feet little promises about what happens when we get back.

### travel_return_loss / 1

Before: I have walked away from work unfinished before. You remember the road back better than the one going in.

After: I'm giving my tired feet little promises about what happens when we get back.

### travel_midleg / 1

Before: I mark the turns when the path gets narrow. Learned that after a very long night going in circles.

After: I can look forward to a journey and still take its purpose seriously.

### travel_response / 1

Before: I am glad you remembered. Someone should.

After: I'm listening. You don't have to make it entertaining.

### travel_response / 2

Before: Tell me the rest when we get home.

After: Take your time. I can enjoy the walk while I hear you.

### travel_hatred / 1

Before: We can share a road. That does not mean I trust you.

After: [annoyed] I would smile a lot more if you fucked off.

### travel_romantic / 1

Before: Stay beside me. I like knowing which footsteps are yours.

After: [softly] I like the little happiness of finding you nearby.

## F11 — Wry

### romantic_response / 2

Before: [softly] I'd like a moment together.

After: [softly] An excellent proposal. A quiet moment with someone I actually want.

### departure / 1

Before: [calm] Checking the straps. Optimism has a terrible load-bearing capacity.

After: [calm] A final check. The road is unlikely to lend me what I forgot.

### return / 1

Before: [playfully] Back in town. Sitting down is looking like an excellent next move.

After: [quietly] Back in town. Sitting down is looking like an excellent next move.

### revived / 1

Before: [quietly] Thank you for getting me back on my feet.

After: [quietly] I meant to thank you for the rescue sooner. Apparently my sincerity needed more recovery time.

### theft / 1

Before: [angry] You took from me. I want it returned.

After: [angry] Give it back. Your talent for making things awkward is already established.

### funeral_general / 1

Before: [quietly] We shared the road. I wish it had ended differently.

After: [quietly] I wish I knew a better thing to do here than speak softly.

### funeral_friendly / 1

Before: [sad] I have nothing clever for this. I just want my friend back.

After: [sad] You knew when the joke was covering something. I'll miss not having to explain.

### funeral_hatred / 1

Before: [flatly] We were bloody awful company for each other.

After: [flatly] We managed very little except getting on each other's nerves.

### funeral_romantic / 1

Before: [sad] There are so many ordinary things I wanted us to do together.

After: [sad] You made room for the things I couldn't turn into a joke. I need that room so badly now.

### dismissal_response / 1

Before: [annoyed] Fine. Keep your fucking space.

After: [flatly] I'll take my fucking commentary to a more appreciative patch of air.

### inquiry_response / 1

Before: [calm] The short version, then. A rare fucking treat.

After: [calm] A few words. Miraculously, I can ration the fucking commentary.

### combat_hatred / 1

Before: [tense] Just what the day needed. Some shit trying to kill me.

After: [tense] Some more shit trying to kill me. How wonderfully predictable.

Before: I learned to read the mile stones before I could read a contract. The distances lie less often.

After: Trees are pleasant company until they start contributing branches to your hair.

Before: The bridge here was condemned before my first job. They still collect the crossing toll.

After: Trees are pleasant company until they start contributing branches to your hair.

After: Trees are pleasant company until they start contributing branches to your hair.

## F13 — Grasping

### general_response / 1

After: [calm] Hello. I'll hear what you have to say before committing my time.

After: [warmly] It's you. I can afford to listen without counting the minutes.

After: [angry] Put my property back where you found it. You haven't earned it by taking it.

After: A harbor is where a small unasked question becomes a large bill.

### travel_navy / 1

Before: I once stood in this queue until the tide changed. The clerk called the departing ship a scheduling error.

After: A harbor is where a small unasked question becomes a large bill.

### travel_ossuary / 1

Before: I have seen the bone carts leave this district. Empty going in, empty coming out. I stopped asking the drivers.

After: Old places make people imagine riches. I imagine the cost of getting them out.

### travel_salt_court / 1

Before: A sailor showed me a coin from this drowned kingdom. Salt had eaten the king away. The crown was still clear.

After: A harbor is where a small unasked question becomes a large bill.

### travel_green_altar / 1

Before: A woodcutter told me he buried his axe here. By spring the handle had put out leaves.

After: Mud gets into seams. I notice anything that shortens the life of good boots.

### travel_birthing_house / 1

Before: I remember when this place had a garden wall. The roots have lifted it clear of the ground.

After: Old places make people imagine riches. I imagine the cost of getting them out.

### travel_low_tide / 1

Before: A fisher once showed me the safe stones. I wrote them down. The sea moved two of them that winter.

After: The sea takes things without even having to negotiate.

### travel_pyre / 1

Before: I used to buy charcoal from this hillside. The burners left when the fires began lighting themselves.

After: Mud gets into seams. I notice anything that shortens the life of good boots.

### travel_maw_boss / 1

Before: I remember these windows dark after supper. Now every room keeps a candle burning. Even the empty ones.

After: I look at locked doors and wonder what makes their owners nervous.

### travel_green_boss / 1

Before: I once watched practice through that gate. Nobody spoke when the last blade stopped. They waited for permission.

After: I'm counting the climb as wear on every possession below my waist.

### travel_law / 1

Before: I used to think a seal meant someone had checked the facts. Now I read the names underneath it.

After: I haven't mistaken agreeing to work for offering unlimited favors.

### travel_criminal / 1

Before: My first quiet delivery came with a promise that nobody would get hurt. I check the locks myself now.

After: I haven't mistaken agreeing to work for offering unlimited favors.

### travel_neutral / 1

Before: I have tracked hungry beasts before. They go where the food is, whatever the contract calls their territory.

After: I haven't mistaken agreeing to work for offering unlimited favors.

### travel_return_win / 1

Before: I used to count the coin before the road home. These days I count the people first.

After: I'm thinking about what will need replacing. I'd prefer to think sitting down.

### travel_return_loss / 1

Before: I have walked away from work unfinished before. You remember the road back better than the one going in.

After: I'm thinking about what will need replacing. I'd prefer to think sitting down.

### travel_midleg / 1

Before: I mark the turns when the path gets narrow. Learned that after a very long night going in circles.

After: I haven't mistaken agreeing to work for offering unlimited favors.

### travel_response / 1

Before: Somebody made money out of that. Somebody always does.

After: I'm paying attention. That doesn't commit me to anything.

### travel_response / 2

Before: Good to know. I would rather learn it at someone else’s expense.

After: I'll hear you out before deciding what it's worth to me.

### travel_hatred / 1

Before: We can share a road. That does not mean I trust you.

After: [annoyed] Your company is a fucking expense I can do without.

### travel_romantic / 1

Before: Stay beside me. I like knowing which footsteps are yours.

After: [softly] You have rather disrupted my careful division of time.

## F14 — Tender

### general_response / 1

Before: [calm] Hello, I'm listening.

After: [calm] Hello. Take a moment if you need one; I'll listen.

### general_response / 4

Before: [calm] I'm glad I could help you.

After: [calm] You're welcome. It matters to me that the help actually helped.

### friendly_response / 1

Before: [warmly] It's good to hear your voice.

After: [warmly] Hearing from you makes the moment feel a little kinder.

### friendly_response / 4

Before: [warmly] You're welcome. I'm glad you're with us.

After: [warmly] You are very welcome. I like knowing I made things easier for a friend.

### romantic_response / 3

Before: [softly] Let's see that we're both ready.

After: [softly] Let's check what we need, love. I like taking care with you.

### romantic_response / 4

Before: [softly] I'm glad I was there for you.

After: [softly] I'm glad I could be the person who helped you this time.

### departure / 1

Before: [calm] Let me check my pack. I can wait while everyone gets ready.

After: [calm] I'll check my things while everyone finds their feet. No need to rush.

### return / 1

Before: [calm] Back at last. I could use a quiet seat and a moment to breathe.

After: [quietly] Home again. I would like a moment to rest properly.

### revived / 1

Before: [quietly] Thank you for getting me back on my feet.

After: [quietly] I remember that you brought me back. I hope I get to make your days a little kinder too.

### theft / 1

Before: [angry] You took from me. I want it returned.

After: [angry] I'd like it back. Being gentle with you doesn't make it yours.

### funeral_general / 1

Before: [quietly] We shared the road. I wish it had ended differently.

After: [quietly] I wish I could have offered you one more small kindness.

### funeral_friendly / 1

Before: [sad] You made room for me in your life. I do not know how to make room for this.

After: [sad] You made it feel safe to need someone. I need my friend very badly now.

### funeral_hatred / 1

Before: [flatly] I could not feel close to you.

After: [flatly] I wish we had been kinder to each other.

### funeral_romantic / 1

Before: [sad] There are so many ordinary things I wanted us to do together.

After: [sad] I keep thinking of things that would make your day easier. I don't know how to stop loving you in those ways.

### dismissal_response / 1

Before: [flatly] All right. I will give you some space.

After: [flatly] I'll give you room without asking you to justify needing it.

### inquiry_response / 1

Before: [calm] Only a moment. I do not want to keep you.

After: [calm] I'll only keep you long enough to say it.

### combat_hatred / 1

Before: [tense] Stay back. I will protect myself, damn it.

After: [tense] Don't come closer, damn it. I will defend myself.

### travel_road / 1

Before: I learned to read the mile stones before I could read a contract. The distances lie less often.

After: I like to keep a pace where nobody needs to apologize for falling behind.

### travel_forest / 1

Before: The bridge here was condemned before my first job. They still collect the crossing toll.

After: I like to keep a pace where nobody needs to apologize for falling behind.

### travel_marsh / 1

Before: I used to follow the lanterns here. Then I noticed they never lit the reeds beneath them.

After: I like to keep a pace where nobody needs to apologize for falling behind.

### travel_ruins / 1

Before: I have seen three different crests cut into these stones. Each owner left the previous name underneath.

After: I want us near enough that nobody has to call very loudly for help.

### travel_crypt / 1

Before: A gravedigger taught me to count the steps down. If the count changes coming back, find another stair.

After: I want us near enough that nobody has to call very loudly for help.

### travel_city / 1

Before: The watch used to chalk safe houses on these doors. Rain made a lot of people suddenly respectable.

After: I notice the small courtesies that let a crowded street keep moving.

### travel_alley / 1

Before: I delivered parcels here once. Every address had two doors, and only one admitted it existed.

After: I notice the small courtesies that let a crowded street keep moving.

### travel_prison / 1

Before: I brought food to a prisoner here. The guard charged me for the bowl on the way out.

After: I notice the small courtesies that let a crowded street keep moving.

### travel_tavern / 1

Before: I learned to ask for the price before ordering here. The chalkboard somehow gets dearer behind your back.

After: I notice the small courtesies that let a crowded street keep moving.

### travel_coast / 1

Before: I once found a road marker under the tide. There was a whole village on the old chart.

After: The shore makes it easier to be quiet with someone.

Before: My first passage cost less than the rope they charged me for touching. Always ask what the fare includes.

After: I always hope the people waiting here get good news.

Before: An old carrier showed me those shelters. Built low for the wind, not because the builders were short.

After: I'll wait if anyone needs to catch their breath. Including me.

After: I notice the small courtesies that let a crowded street keep moving.

After: I like to keep a pace where nobody needs to apologize for falling behind.

After: I want us near enough that nobody has to call very loudly for help.

After: I notice the small courtesies that let a crowded street keep moving.

### general_response / 1

After: I startle at branches. At least they rarely take offense.

### travel_maw_boss / 1

Before: I remember these windows dark after supper. Now every room keeps a candle burning. Even the empty ones.

After: Someone passing too close sets my nerves going for another street.

### travel_green_boss / 1

After: I'll let my feet concentrate while the rest of me worries.

After: I repeat the task in my head. It keeps less helpful thoughts busy.

After: I repeat the task in my head. It keeps less helpful thoughts busy.

After: I think I'll feel better once the things around me look familiar again.

## F17 — Dramatic

### friendly_response / 1

Before: [warmly] A welcome voice! I'm listening.

After: [warmly] Ah, a familiar voice! I can put the performance down a moment.

### romantic_response / 4

Before: [softly] I'm glad I could be beside you.

After: [softly] Helping you meant more than making any grand promise about it.

### departure / 1

Before: [calm] A final inspection of my belongings before the grand departure.

After: [calm] An inspection before departure. I prefer drama that isn't caused by forgotten supplies.

### return / 1

After: [quietly] We have returned. My next grand gesture will be putting the pack down.

### revived / 1

Before: [quietly] Thank you for getting me back on my feet.

After: [quietly] I owe you thanks for my return. I shall resist making them elaborate enough to obscure the feeling.

### theft / 1

Before: [angry] You took from me. I want it returned.

After: [angry] This is theft, not an intriguing turn in our relationship. Return it.

### funeral_general / 1

Before: [quietly] We shared the road. I wish it had ended differently.

After: [quietly] I won't fill the silence just because silence frightens me.

### funeral_friendly / 1

Before: [sad] I rehearsed something. It sounds dreadful now. I loved being your friend.

After: [sad] You let me be ordinary with you. I hadn't known how much I needed that.

### funeral_hatred / 1

Before: [flatly] I will not perform grief I do not feel.

After: [flatly] We had no friendship beneath the performance.

### funeral_romantic / 1

After: [sad] I thought grief would arrive with words. It keeps arriving as the moment I'd turn to show you something.

### dismissal_response / 1

Before: [flatly] All right. I will give you some space.

After: [flatly] I'll leave the scene before it becomes a siege.

### inquiry_response / 1

After: [calm] One small speech. I promise to respect the word small.

### combat_hatred / 1

After: [tense] A fucking hostile reception. I can answer that.

After: The woods offer mystery, beauty, and nowhere sensible to put a trailing sleeve.

After: The woods offer mystery, beauty, and nowhere sensible to put a trailing sleeve.

After: The woods offer mystery, beauty, and nowhere sensible to put a trailing sleeve.

After: This place has presence. I would like it to have better lighting.

After: This place has presence. I would like it to have better lighting.

After: A city never knows when to end a scene. Someone is always entering.

After: The woods offer mystery, beauty, and nowhere sensible to put a trailing sleeve.

After: A city never knows when to end a scene. Someone is always entering.

After: A city gives me plenty of chances to be somebody's bad influence.

### travel_tavern / 1

Before: I learned to ask for the price before ordering here. The chalkboard somehow gets dearer behind your back.

After: A city gives me plenty of chances to be somebody's bad influence.

### travel_coast / 1

Before: I once found a road marker under the tide. There was a whole village on the old chart.

After: This wind makes me want to shout. I rarely need the encouragement.

### travel_port / 1

Before: My first passage cost less than the rope they charged me for touching. Always ask what the fare includes.

After: Harbors make reckless ideas sound as if they have schedules.

### travel_mountain / 1

Before: An old carrier showed me those shelters. Built low for the wind, not because the builders were short.

After: I can handle the climb. Let me bitch about it while I do.

### travel_maw / 1

Before: I once waited here for a shirt to dry. Three people collected parcels. Nobody brought any washing.

After: A city gives me plenty of chances to be somebody's bad influence.

### travel_antler / 1

Before: I have crossed this toll gate under three companies. They change the banner and keep the same collector.

After: I like a woodland ramble. The scratches are usually worth the story.

### travel_academy / 1

Before: I carried ink to the Academy once. They inspected the bottles more carefully than the man carrying them.

After: I'm going to look brave until I have a good reason to look elsewhere.

### travel_bell / 1

Before: I bought paper here once. The shopkeeper knew who had sent me before I mentioned a name.

After: A city gives me plenty of chances to be somebody's bad influence.

### travel_green / 1

Before: I watched a recruit sweep these steps in the rain. His teacher said the leaves were not the lesson.

After: I can handle the climb. Let me bitch about it while I do.

### travel_tally / 1

Before: I saw a Tally crew divide a broken compass into shares. Nobody wanted it; nobody would waive their portion.

After: Harbors make reckless ideas sound as if they have schedules.

### travel_navy / 1

Before: I once stood in this queue until the tide changed. The clerk called the departing ship a scheduling error.

After: Harbors make reckless ideas sound as if they have schedules.

### travel_ossuary / 1

Before: I have seen the bone carts leave this district. Empty going in, empty coming out. I stopped asking the drivers.

After: I'm going to look brave until I have a good reason to look elsewhere.

Before: A sailor showed me a coin from this drowned kingdom. Salt had eaten the king away. The crown was still clear.

After: Harbors make reckless ideas sound as if they have schedules.

### travel_green_altar / 1

Before: A woodcutter told me he buried his axe here. By spring the handle had put out leaves.

After: I like a woodland ramble. The scratches are usually worth the story.

### travel_birthing_house / 1

Before: I remember when this place had a garden wall. The roots have lifted it clear of the ground.

After: I'm going to look brave until I have a good reason to look elsewhere.

### travel_low_tide / 1

Before: A fisher once showed me the safe stones. I wrote them down. The sea moved two of them that winter.

After: This wind makes me want to shout. I rarely need the encouragement.

### travel_pyre / 1

Before: I used to buy charcoal from this hillside. The burners left when the fires began lighting themselves.

After: I like a woodland ramble. The scratches are usually worth the story.

### travel_maw_boss / 1

Before: I remember these windows dark after supper. Now every room keeps a candle burning. Even the empty ones.

After: A city gives me plenty of chances to be somebody's bad influence.

### travel_green_boss / 1

Before: I once watched practice through that gate. Nobody spoke when the last blade stopped. They waited for permission.

After: I can handle the climb. Let me bitch about it while I do.

### travel_law / 1

Before: I used to think a seal meant someone had checked the facts. Now I read the names underneath it.

After: I've read the terms. Reckless doesn't have to mean fucking uninformed.

### travel_criminal / 1

Before: My first quiet delivery came with a promise that nobody would get hurt. I check the locks myself now.

After: I've read the terms. Reckless doesn't have to mean fucking uninformed.

### travel_neutral / 1

Before: I have tracked hungry beasts before. They go where the food is, whatever the contract calls their territory.

After: I've read the terms. Reckless doesn't have to mean fucking uninformed.

### travel_return_win / 1

Before: I used to count the coin before the road home. These days I count the people first.

After: I'm ready to stop pretending this pack and I enjoy each other's company.

### travel_return_loss / 1

Before: I have walked away from work unfinished before. You remember the road back better than the one going in.

After: I'm ready to stop pretending this pack and I enjoy each other's company.

### travel_midleg / 1

Before: I mark the turns when the path gets narrow. Learned that after a very long night going in circles.

After: I've read the terms. Reckless doesn't have to mean fucking uninformed.

### travel_response / 1

Before: I will remember that. Preferably without repeating the mistake.

After: You've got my ear. Try not to look so fucking astonished.

### travel_response / 2

Before: There is always something they leave out of the stories.

After: Go on. I'll give somebody else a turn at being interesting.

### travel_hatred / 1

Before: We can share a road. That does not mean I trust you.

After: [annoyed] You are a miserable fucking use of perfectly good air.

### travel_romantic / 1

Before: Stay beside me. I like knowing which footsteps are yours.

After: [softly] I like how close I can get to you without needing an excuse.

## F27 — Elegant

### general_response / 1

Before: [calm] Hello. Please go on.

After: [calm] Hello. I am quite content to listen for a while.

### general_response / 2

Before: [calm] I'd enjoy a little company.

After: [calm] A little company would make the pause more agreeable.

### general_response / 4

Before: [calm] I'm pleased it was helpful.

After: [calm] You're welcome. I am pleased the effort served its purpose.

### friendly_response / 2

Before: [warmly] I'd like that very much.

After: [warmly] I'd enjoy sitting together without having to make an occasion of it.

### romantic_response / 1

Before: [softly] There you are, darling.

After: [softly] Darling, you have made it very easy to set everything else aside.

### romantic_response / 2

Before: [softly] I'd love a quiet moment together.

After: [softly] A quiet moment with you sounds like something to savor.

### romantic_response / 3

Before: [softly] Let's see that we're both ready.

After: [softly] Let's give our preparations a little care before turning to each other.

### romantic_response / 4

Before: [softly] I'm glad I could be beside you.

After: [softly] I was glad to offer you something more useful than sympathy, love.

### revived / 1

Before: [quietly] Thank you for getting me back on my feet.

After: [quietly] I wanted a quiet moment to thank you for bringing me back. There is no graceful way to make that seem small.

### theft / 1

Before: [angry] You took from me. I want it returned.

After: [angry] Please return what you took. Courtesy doesn't obscure what happened.

### funeral_general / 1

Before: [quietly] We shared the road. I wish it had ended differently.

After: [quietly] I hope you were seen with affection in moments strangers never witnessed.

### funeral_romantic / 1

Before: [sad] There are so many ordinary things I wanted us to do together.

After: [sad] I keep seeing something beautiful and turning to share it. I haven't learned the shape of your absence.

### dismissal_response / 1

After: [flatly] I'll leave you to enjoy the quiet you've requested.

After: I enjoy the green light under trees. I make allowances for what it does to hems.

After: I enjoy the green light under trees. I make allowances for what it does to hems.

After: I enjoy the green light under trees. I make allowances for what it does to hems.

After: I can appreciate old stone without touching everything that has survived this long.

After: I check a street name before assuming the directions were accurate.

### travel_green / 1

Before: I watched a recruit sweep these steps in the rain. His teacher said the leaves were not the lesson.

After: I prefer a measured pace to repeatedly discovering I went too fast.

### travel_tally / 1

Before: I saw a Tally crew divide a broken compass into shares. Nobody wanted it; nobody would waive their portion.

After: I ask exactly what passage includes. Approximately is an expensive word.

### travel_navy / 1

Before: I once stood in this queue until the tide changed. The clerk called the departing ship a scheduling error.

After: I ask exactly what passage includes. Approximately is an expensive word.

### travel_ossuary / 1

Before: I have seen the bone carts leave this district. Empty going in, empty coming out. I stopped asking the drivers.

After: I count the turns. Similar-looking passages are no reason to guess.

### travel_salt_court / 1

Before: A sailor showed me a coin from this drowned kingdom. Salt had eaten the king away. The crown was still clear.

After: I ask exactly what passage includes. Approximately is an expensive word.

### travel_green_altar / 1

Before: A woodcutter told me he buried his axe here. By spring the handle had put out leaves.

After: I like to know whether a bend in the path matches the route we were given.

### travel_birthing_house / 1

Before: I remember when this place had a garden wall. The roots have lifted it clear of the ground.

After: I count the turns. Similar-looking passages are no reason to guess.

### travel_low_tide / 1

Before: A fisher once showed me the safe stones. I wrote them down. The sea moved two of them that winter.

After: I want to know where the water reaches, not merely where it is now.

### travel_pyre / 1

Before: I used to buy charcoal from this hillside. The burners left when the fires began lighting themselves.

After: I like to know whether a bend in the path matches the route we were given.

### travel_maw_boss / 1

Before: I remember these windows dark after supper. Now every room keeps a candle burning. Even the empty ones.

After: I check a street name before assuming the directions were accurate.

### travel_green_boss / 1

Before: I once watched practice through that gate. Nobody spoke when the last blade stopped. They waited for permission.

After: I prefer a measured pace to repeatedly discovering I went too fast.

### travel_law / 1

Before: I used to think a seal meant someone had checked the facts. Now I read the names underneath it.

After: I distinguish what was promised from what I merely assumed.

### travel_criminal / 1

Before: My first quiet delivery came with a promise that nobody would get hurt. I check the locks myself now.

After: I distinguish what was promised from what I merely assumed.

### travel_neutral / 1

Before: I have tracked hungry beasts before. They go where the food is, whatever the contract calls their territory.

After: I distinguish what was promised from what I merely assumed.

### travel_return_win / 1

Before: I used to count the coin before the road home. These days I count the people first.

After: I will take stock once we're back and I can do it without walking.

### travel_return_loss / 1

Before: I have walked away from work unfinished before. You remember the road back better than the one going in.

After: I will take stock once we're back and I can do it without walking.

### travel_midleg / 1

Before: I mark the turns when the path gets narrow. Learned that after a very long night going in circles.

After: I distinguish what was promised from what I merely assumed.

### travel_response / 1

Before: Useful. Keep your eyes on the road.

After: I'll hear the complete account before drawing a conclusion.

### travel_response / 2

Before: That is how you learn. Once should be enough.

After: I'm listening for what you mean, not just the phrasing.

### travel_hatred / 1

Before: We can share a road. That does not mean I trust you.

After: [annoyed] I have measured my patience, and your bullshit exceeds it.

### travel_romantic / 1

Before: Stay beside me. I like knowing which footsteps are yours.

After: [softly] I like how little correction a moment with you requires.

## F30 — Bereaved

### general_response / 4

Before: [calm] I'm glad I could help a little.

After: [calm] I'm glad I could make a small difference where one was needed.

### friendly_response / 1

Before: [warmly] Good to hear your voice.

After: [warmly] It's good to have your voice here in the day.

### friendly_response / 2

Before: [warmly] I'd welcome your company.

After: [warmly] I would like some company that doesn't require me to explain the quiet.

### hatred_response / 3

Before: [flatly] Please ask someone else to check.

After: [flatly] I haven't the heart to help with your preparations. Ask someone else.

### romantic_response / 1

Before: [softly] There you are, love.

After: [softly] There you are, love. I'm glad we have this moment while it's here.

### romantic_response / 3

Before: [softly] Let's see that we're both ready.

After: [softly] Let's check things together. It gives us a little care to share.

### romantic_response / 4

Before: [softly] I'm glad we have more time together.

After: [softly] I'm grateful I could help you, and that you're here to tell me.

### revived / 1

Before: [quietly] Thank you for getting me back on my feet.

After: [quietly] You brought me back when I might have become another absence. I am grateful, even when it is difficult to say.

### theft / 1

Before: [angry] You took from me. I want it returned.

After: [angry] Please return it. I can't bear people deciding what I should lose.

### funeral_general / 1

Before: [quietly] We shared the road. I wish it had ended differently.

After: [quietly] I know a little of the space you will leave. I'm sorry for those who know it well.

### funeral_romantic / 1

Before: [sad] There are so many ordinary things I wanted us to do together.

After: [sad] I knew love could end in this. I would still choose our time together. I just wanted more.

### dismissal_response / 1

Before: [flatly] All right. I will give you some space.

After: [flatly] I'll leave you alone. I know what it is to need that.

### travel_road / 1

Before: I learned to read the mile stones before I could read a contract. The distances lie less often.

After: I like the way a path can occupy my feet while my thoughts go elsewhere.

### travel_forest / 1

Before: The bridge here was condemned before my first job. They still collect the crossing toll.

After: I like the way a path can occupy my feet while my thoughts go elsewhere.

### travel_marsh / 1

Before: I used to follow the lanterns here. Then I noticed they never lit the reeds beneath them.

After: I like the way a path can occupy my feet while my thoughts go elsewhere.

### travel_ruins / 1

Before: I have seen three different crests cut into these stones. Each owner left the previous name underneath.

After: I don't need the dark to remind me what absence feels like.

### travel_crypt / 1

Before: A gravedigger taught me to count the steps down. If the count changes coming back, find another stair.

After: I don't need the dark to remind me what absence feels like.

### travel_city / 1

Before: The watch used to chalk safe houses on these doors. Rain made a lot of people suddenly respectable.

After: Sometimes an ordinary street reminds me of someone. I never know which one will.

### travel_alley / 1

Before: I delivered parcels here once. Every address had two doors, and only one admitted it existed.

After: Sometimes an ordinary street reminds me of someone. I never know which one will.

### travel_prison / 1

Before: I brought food to a prisoner here. The guard charged me for the bowl on the way out.

After: Sometimes an ordinary street reminds me of someone. I never know which one will.

### travel_tavern / 1

Before: I learned to ask for the price before ordering here. The chalkboard somehow gets dearer behind your back.

After: Sometimes an ordinary street reminds me of someone. I never know which one will.

### travel_coast / 1

Before: I once found a road marker under the tide. There was a whole village on the old chart.

After: I watch the water come back and try not to ask that of other things.

### travel_port / 1

Before: My first passage cost less than the rope they charged me for touching. Always ask what the fare includes.

After: I find departures difficult. Watching the work gives me somewhere to put my eyes.

### travel_mountain / 1

Before: An old carrier showed me those shelters. Built low for the wind, not because the builders were short.

After: The climb makes me attend to the next step. There is kindness in that.

Before: I once waited here for a shirt to dry. Three people collected parcels. Nobody brought any washing.

After: Sometimes an ordinary street reminds me of someone. I never know which one will.

After: I like the way a path can occupy my feet while my thoughts go elsewhere.

After: I wish people would leave a little room when they pass.

After: I wish people would leave a little room when they pass.

After: I wish people would leave a little room when they pass.

After: I like the shore better when I can see where the water ends.

After: I never know where to stand without being in somebody's way.

After: [softly] I like how close we can sit without needing to explain anything.

After: [flatly] I shall leave you to the company you prefer.

After: I used to like long walks. Now I like knowing when they end.

### travel_forest / 1

Before: The bridge here was condemned before my first job. They still collect the crossing toll.

After: I used to like long walks. Now I like knowing when they end.

### travel_marsh / 1

Before: I used to follow the lanterns here. Then I noticed they never lit the reeds beneath them.

After: I used to like long walks. Now I like knowing when they end.

### travel_ruins / 1

Before: I have seen three different crests cut into these stones. Each owner left the previous name underneath.

After: I would find old stone more restful with a little more daylight.

### travel_crypt / 1

Before: A gravedigger taught me to count the steps down. If the count changes coming back, find another stair.

After: I would find old stone more restful with a little more daylight.

### travel_city / 1

Before: The watch used to chalk safe houses on these doors. Rain made a lot of people suddenly respectable.

After: Town roads are kinder to tired feet than they get credit for.

### travel_alley / 1

Before: I delivered parcels here once. Every address had two doors, and only one admitted it existed.

After: Town roads are kinder to tired feet than they get credit for.

### travel_prison / 1

Before: I brought food to a prisoner here. The guard charged me for the bowl on the way out.

After: Town roads are kinder to tired feet than they get credit for.

### travel_tavern / 1

Before: I learned to ask for the price before ordering here. The chalkboard somehow gets dearer behind your back.

After: Town roads are kinder to tired feet than they get credit for.

### travel_coast / 1

Before: I once found a road marker under the tide. There was a whole village on the old chart.

After: The sea can do the restless moving for both of us.

### travel_port / 1

Before: My first passage cost less than the rope they charged me for touching. Always ask what the fare includes.

After: I enjoy watching a boat leave when I am not required to chase it.

### travel_mountain / 1

Before: An old carrier showed me those shelters. Built low for the wind, not because the builders were short.

After: No hurry. The summit has had longer to get ready than I have.

### travel_maw / 1

Before: I once waited here for a shirt to dry. Three people collected parcels. Nobody brought any washing.

After: Town roads are kinder to tired feet than they get credit for.

### travel_antler / 1

Before: I have crossed this toll gate under three companies. They change the banner and keep the same collector.

After: I used to like long walks. Now I like knowing when they end.

### travel_academy / 1

Before: I carried ink to the Academy once. They inspected the bottles more carefully than the man carrying them.

After: I would find old stone more restful with a little more daylight.

### travel_bell / 1

Before: I bought paper here once. The shopkeeper knew who had sent me before I mentioned a name.

After: Town roads are kinder to tired feet than they get credit for.

### travel_green / 1

Before: I watched a recruit sweep these steps in the rain. His teacher said the leaves were not the lesson.

After: No hurry. The summit has had longer to get ready than I have.

### travel_tally / 1

Before: I saw a Tally crew divide a broken compass into shares. Nobody wanted it; nobody would waive their portion.

After: I enjoy watching a boat leave when I am not required to chase it.

### travel_navy / 1

Before: I once stood in this queue until the tide changed. The clerk called the departing ship a scheduling error.

After: I enjoy watching a boat leave when I am not required to chase it.

### travel_ossuary / 1

Before: I have seen the bone carts leave this district. Empty going in, empty coming out. I stopped asking the drivers.

After: I would find old stone more restful with a little more daylight.

### travel_salt_court / 1

Before: A sailor showed me a coin from this drowned kingdom. Salt had eaten the king away. The crown was still clear.

After: I enjoy watching a boat leave when I am not required to chase it.

### travel_green_altar / 1

Before: A woodcutter told me he buried his axe here. By spring the handle had put out leaves.

After: I used to like long walks. Now I like knowing when they end.

### travel_birthing_house / 1

Before: I remember when this place had a garden wall. The roots have lifted it clear of the ground.

After: I would find old stone more restful with a little more daylight.

### travel_low_tide / 1

Before: A fisher once showed me the safe stones. I wrote them down. The sea moved two of them that winter.

After: The sea can do the restless moving for both of us.

### travel_pyre / 1

Before: I used to buy charcoal from this hillside. The burners left when the fires began lighting themselves.

After: I used to like long walks. Now I like knowing when they end.

### travel_maw_boss / 1

Before: I remember these windows dark after supper. Now every room keeps a candle burning. Even the empty ones.

After: Town roads are kinder to tired feet than they get credit for.

### travel_green_boss / 1

Before: I once watched practice through that gate. Nobody spoke when the last blade stopped. They waited for permission.

After: No hurry. The summit has had longer to get ready than I have.

### travel_law / 1

Before: I used to think a seal meant someone had checked the facts. Now I read the names underneath it.

After: I intend to spend my effort on the actual work, if the world permits.

### travel_criminal / 1

Before: My first quiet delivery came with a promise that nobody would get hurt. I check the locks myself now.

After: I intend to spend my effort on the actual work, if the world permits.

### travel_neutral / 1

Before: I have tracked hungry beasts before. They go where the food is, whatever the contract calls their territory.

After: I intend to spend my effort on the actual work, if the world permits.

### travel_return_win / 1

Before: I used to count the coin before the road home. These days I count the people first.

After: I'm saving a little energy for the last part of getting back.

### travel_return_loss / 1

Before: I have walked away from work unfinished before. You remember the road back better than the one going in.

After: I'm saving a little energy for the last part of getting back.

### travel_midleg / 1

Before: I mark the turns when the path gets narrow. Learned that after a very long night going in circles.

After: I intend to spend my effort on the actual work, if the world permits.

### travel_response / 1

Before: Funny what stays with you after a job.

After: I'm listening. Answering may take a moment.

### travel_response / 2

Before: I know the sort of lesson. You keep paying for it.

After: Go on, if you've the breath for it.

### travel_hatred / 1

Before: We can share a road. That does not mean I trust you.

After: [annoyed] Go exhaust someone else's fucking patience.

### travel_romantic / 1

Before: Stay beside me. I like knowing which footsteps are yours.

After: [softly] I like being able to rest beside you without explanations.
